市場焦點
美國
• 10月S&P Global綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖較前月回落，但仍處高檔區間；服務業動
能略為放緩，製造業PMI則升至56.0，創近4年半新高。新訂單增速加快 ，顯示企業需求、投
資及庫存回補動能仍具韌性 ，然而企業售價與投入成本同步上升 ，反映需求改善 、供應鏈延
遲及成本轉嫁效應持續存在 ，通膨降溫進程仍具不確定性 。整體而言 ，市場關注焦點轉向通
膨與利率走勢，高利率環境恐維持較長時間，進而推高股債資產評價波動。
中國
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季 GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期 ，在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，
短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建議聚焦在
半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費 、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，

仍需觀察後續刺激政策與消費信心回升情況。
法國
政局動盪
• 法國金融市場因各地抗議活動持續擴大 ，面臨股債雙跌的局面。市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支
出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮 。法國10年期公債殖利率一度突破 5%，法德利差亦擴
大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標仍處於歷史相對低檔 ，遠低於歐
元區主權債務危機期間，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務危機的風險仍相對有限。

美國PMI維持強勁擴張，經濟成長續航但通膨壓力再升溫
• 企業需求強勁支撐美國景氣維持高檔擴張 ：美國10月綜合PMI初值由58.4降至57.6，雖略低於前月，但仍處於近五年來
的高位區間，顯示企業活動持續高速擴張；其中服務業 PMI為57.9、製造業PMI升至56.0並創53個月新高，新訂單增速
更創逾4年半最佳表現，反映內需穩健、出口需求改善與企業補庫存效應同步發酵，美國第四季經濟動能仍具韌性。
• 成本上升與供應瓶頸推升通膨風險： 企業售價漲幅創2022年8月以來最高，能源、運輸、金屬原料及薪資成本同步上揚 ，
加上供應鏈交期延長與訂單積壓擴大，使企業得以持續轉嫁成本，暗示未來數月消費者通膨可能再度偏離聯準會目標區
間，市場對利率維持較高水準的預期恐因此升溫。
• 後市觀察：景氣韌性有利獲利成長，但通膨可能推升殖利率、壓抑高評價資產。投資配置建議優先布局美國大型科技股 、

美國金融股及工業資本支出受惠族群，可透過NASDAQ 100 ETF、S&P 500 ETF及美國銀行股ETF參與企業獲利成長；
固定收益則以短天期投資級債為主，以降低利率波動風險。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.24

海外資金流入美國市場，支撐美國資產吸引力
• 海外資金持續流入美國：美國財政部8月TIC資料顯示，
整體資金淨流入 1,998億美元，其中民間部門淨流入
1,785億美元，為主要資金來源；外資淨買進美國長
期證券3,007億美元，遠高於美國投資人加碼海外資
產216億美元，反映全球資金在經濟成長與流動性考
量下，對美國金融市場仍維持高度的配置需求。
• 全球資金偏好美國資產： 外資 8月淨買入美國股票
1,319億美元，高於美國公債與公司債 ，顯示海外投
資人仍看好美國企業獲利與經濟基本面 ，雖然短期國
庫券持有量下降，部分跨境美元資金亦出現波動，但
以10月近況來看長天期公債標售仍吸引境外央行 、
保險資金及大型資產管理機構參與，反映高殖利率水
準對長期資金仍具吸引力。在經濟成長維持韌性與企

業獲利支撐下，美國資產仍為全球資金配置的重要核
心市場。
• 市場看法：海外資金流入有助反映美國資產吸引力，
惟後續仍須觀察通膨 、Fed政策及企業獲利表現 。投
資布局建議可以美國投資等級債、美國中短天期公債
及大型AI基礎建設受惠族群為核心配置 ，兼顧收益、
防禦及長期成長潛力。

歷史數據顯示全球股市突破歷史新高後，後續上漲機率高
• 很多人直覺以為見高就是頂，但過去20年的統計資料顯示，全球股市在創下新高後，多數時間選擇繼續向上走。
• 勝率顯著偏高： 全球股市 (MSCI全球指數 )創新高後持有一年 ，獲得正報酬的機率高達 62%，三個月後的正報酬機率更達
71%。美股與台股表現最亮眼，標普500指數創高後一年上漲機率為76%，那斯達克為79%；而台灣股市表現更為驚人，創
高後三個月內上漲的機率高達82%。
• 下檔拉回風險有限：在創高後短期(一個月內)修正幅度其實相當溫和 ，全球多數主要市場的回徹幅度中位數僅 1%～2%左右。
• 突破前高優於連續創新高： 若市場在歷經至少一個月以上的整理後突破前高 ，其未來一年的平均報酬率高達 8.4%，遠高於

連續每天創新高的3.9%。這代表市場經適度整理後的突破，具備更健康的上漲動能。
創新高後的平均遠期報酬率
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.22

顧
市場寬度收窄，由少數巨頭獨撐
• 當前的市場特徵由少數巨頭帶頭衝： 科技股權重較高的指數(如那斯達克、台股、韓股)，長期以來都有少數權重股拉動指數
的現象。自2024年以來，全球13個主要市場中有10個市場在創新高時，參與上漲的股票比例明顯下降 。已開發市場創新高
時高於200日均線的股票比例從過去 75.5%降至65.1%，新興市場更降至 61.3%。這意味著指數看似風光 ，但多數個股並未
同步創高，當漲勢過度集中於少數大型科技巨頭時，整體市場的抗風險能力會相對減弱。
• 小結與投資建議：歷史經驗顯示，創新高本身是強勢市場的特徵，後續繼續走揚的機率遠大於立刻崩盤。然而，鑑於目前市
場寬度收窄、權重高度集中於科技股的現狀 ，盲目追高個股的風險已顯著增加 。投資建議可以利用ETF掌握大盤趨勢，由於

指數上漲主要由龍頭股驅動 ，透過市值型ETF(如追蹤標普500ETF)能直接享受龍頭巨頭帶來的指數創高紅利 ，避免選錯個股
而賺了指數、賠了價差。此外，提防短期股市回檔，可運用定期定額分批布局或等待大盤適度拉回整理再行加碼，降低追高
風險。
創新高時不同水準的市場寬度佔比
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.22
已開發市場和新興市場創新高時的寬度

法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓
• 法國政局動盪加劇財政壓力，股債同步承壓：法國各地抗議活動持續擴大，最初由學生發起，隨後因生活成本高漲而引發其
他群體響應。在財政空間有限、俄烏戰爭與美伊衝突推升能源價格的背景下，政府難以有效緩解民生成本壓力，法國金融市
場因此面臨股債雙跌的局面。
• 公債殖利率攀升，但系統性風險仍相對可控：市場憂心法國政府可能擴大支出以平息抗議，加劇財政可持續性疑慮。法國10
年期公債殖利率一度突破5%，法德利差亦擴大至歐債危機以來高點。不過，歐洲央行系統性壓力綜合指標（CISS）仍處於
歷史相對低檔，遠低於歐元區主權債務危機期間，顯示金融市場尚未出現廣泛的系統性壓力，短期內演變為新一輪主權債務
危機的風險仍相對有限。
G7社會動盪指數，法國高居榜首

資料來源： Beco models country，2026.10.19 資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.19

資料來源： Bloomberg，2026.10.15 資料來源： tradingeconomics，2026.10.21
日本貿易收支
• 日本貿易逆差擴大，進口增幅高於出口：日本2026年9月貿易逆差擴大至5,366億日圓，較去年同期的2,777億日圓增加93%，
高於市場預期的1,810億日圓，連續第五個月呈現貿易赤字。出口年增16.7%至10.97兆日圓，受惠於半導體相關電子零件、
製造設備及汽車出口成長；惟進口年增18.9%至11.51兆日圓，增幅高於出口，主要受到原油、半導體電子零件及液化天然
氣進口增加帶動。其中，原油進口年增51.1%，部分反映日本在荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張下，積極分散能源採購來源。
• 日本央行本週料維持利率不變：日本央行9月將政策利率調升25個基點至1.25%，創31年來新高。考量前幾次升息對經濟的

傳導效果仍待觀察，加上貿易逆差擴大反映進口成本壓力，多數分析師預期日本央行本週將暫停升息，以評估貨幣緊縮對經
濟與通膨的影響。

中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期
• 中國國家統計局公布第三季GDP年增率4.5%，優於市場預期：顯示中國經濟在第二季落底後呈現溫和回升 ，從季度表現來看，
一、二、三季增速分別為5.0%、4.3%及4.5%，反映政策支持與外需復甦開始發揮效果 ，全年達成官方4.5%成長目標的機率
明顯提升，第三季經濟改善的主要動能來自出口 ，受惠於全球企業持續擴建 AI基礎設施，及科技產品需求強勁 ，中國高科技
產品出口表現亮眼 ，尤其是積體電路 、伺服器、電腦零組件，及儲存設備等產品增幅顯著 ，高技術製造業維持雙位數成長 。
同時，中國對美國及歐盟出口維持強勁 ，推升貿易順差 走升，為支撐經濟的重要力量 。然而，內需疲弱仍是當前經濟最大隱
憂，房地產調整尚未結束，居民消費與企業投資意願保守，使經濟復甦結構呈現「外熱內冷」的特徵。

• 展望後市：在出口與科技製造業帶動下 ，短期成長可望維持穩定 ，但若內需未能有效改善 ，經濟成長動能恐難全面擴散 ，建
議聚焦在半導體、AI硬體、伺服器供應鏈 ，及高端製造相關產業 ，至於消費、房地產及傳統內需類股 ，仍需觀察後續刺激政
策與消費信心回升情況。
資料來源：開源證券，2026.10.22

彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高
• 彭博預估騰訊、阿里巴巴在中國AI競爭中勝出機率較高：彭博產業研究認為，中國AI產業未來將由大型科技平台主導 ，其中騰
訊、字節跳動與阿里巴巴憑藉龐大用戶基礎 、數據資源、資金實力及完整生態系統 ，在AI競賽中具備明顯優勢 ，這些企業可將
AI廣泛應用於電商、社群、遊戲、金融與雲端業務，提升營運效率並強化競爭門檻，例如：阿里巴巴利用 AI 驅動電商平台上的
個人化產品推薦，而騰訊則運用 AI 優化短影音廣告並強化微信功能。騰訊自 2026 年 7 月起開始將混元 AI 模型整合至其電子
遊戲中，使遊戲角色及助理能更準確地回應玩家 ，然而，AI模型訓練與基礎建設投入龐大 ，獲利能力短期仍受壓抑 ，且須面對
監管、資料隱私及晶片供應等挑戰 。此外，智譜、月之暗面、MiniMax及商湯等AI新創雖具技術創新能力，但缺乏規模效益、

穩定現金流及長期資本支持，未來獲利挑戰較大。
• 投資建議：看好中國AI長期發展者，可優先關注具資金 、算力與應用場景優勢的大型平台企業 ，受惠程度可望高於多數 AI新創
公司。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.21

台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高，台股第四季有望先蹲後跳
• 台灣9月外銷訂單再創新高： 9月外銷訂單1,142.1億美元，連兩個月破千億美元 ，再創單月新高；年增率高達 62.4%，連續
20個月呈雙位數正成長 。經濟部表示，AI強勁需求超乎預期 ，預估今年第四季接單還會優於第三季 。展望10月表現，外銷
訂單將持續亮麗，估落在1,125億至1,159億美元，有望連三個月破千億美元。
• 台股第四季有望先蹲後跳： 10月初以來，台股在盤中觸及歷史高點 49,968點後面臨一波修正 ，主要反映在新一輪的財報公
布前，市場觀望AI投資是否存在泡沫化風險 。短期來看，台股在季線附近出現支撐 ，不過在11月初的美國期中選舉落幕前 ，
台股維持區間震盪的可能性較高；中期來看，美國期中選後往往因政治不確定性下降，歷史統計股市上漲機率高，加上台灣

年底集團作帳及選舉行情，正向看待年底台股表現。
資料來源：經濟部統計處，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.20。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

### Page 1

Macquarie Equity Research
26 October 2026
Nan Ya Plastics
Mix upgrade accelerating
Key Points
• NPC reported Sep revenue up 55% YoY while net profit surged over 9x
YoY to TWD35.1bn, with Sep DPS above 2Q26 DPS.
• We expect a rising high-end product contribution to accelerate Low Dk/
Low CTE glass fibre sales next year.
• Our industry checks suggest that CCL supply will remain tight due to
glass fibre shortages.
• Upside to our 4Q26 forecast. NPC reported strong Sep earnings
following a required disclosure on unusual share-price movements.
Revenue rose 55% YoY to TWD34.4bn, while net profit surged over
9x to TWD35.1bn. Sep EPS reached TWD4.42, above 2Q26 quarterly
EPS of TWD3.37. Mgmt attributed the strength mainly to electronic
materials, including high-end CCL benefiting from AI demand. We
estimate Sept EBIT at TWD8–9bn, ~45% of 3Q26 EBIT, implying upside to
our TWD20.3bn 4Q EBIT estimate.
• Rising high-end CCL contribution in 2027/28. The company refuted
market rumours that its M9 material had failed customer validation,
noting that advanced CCL expansion remains on track, with new capacity
fully dedicated to high-end products. We expect stronger growth in Low
Dk/Low CTE glass fibres, with high-end products reaching 40% of the mix
vs 10% this year as CSP orders ramp up next year. We also expect M9
CCL to pass validation in 1H27.
• Q-Cloth and PTFE not ready yet. Our checks at Taiwan TPCA Show
2026 with glass fibre and CCL players suggest demand is multiple times
industry capacity, with glass-fibre supply the key bottleneck given long
capacity and weaving-machine ramp-up times. As producers shift
capacity towards Low Dk/T-Cloth, E-Cloth supply should remain tight. Q-
Cloth remains constrained by high costs and poor flexibility, while Low
Dk glass fibre with HC Resin can deliver similar performance. PTFE is
also unlikely to threaten glass fibre near term given its low mechanical
strength.
Earnings changes: No changes.
Valuation: We maintain our NT$506.00 TP based on an unchanged 20x
FY27E PER.
Catalysts: 2H26 high-end CCL ramp-up and capacity expansion, glass fibre
capacity additions, and stronger equity-method earnings from 2027.
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
We expect high-end CCL qualifications, mix upgrades and utilisation gains
to drive earnings growth, supported by recovering legacy businesses and
higher associate earnings.

Macquarie Equity Research Nan Ya Plastics
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Slower-than-expected AI data centre capex.
• Delays in, or failure of, high-end CCL qualification.
• Weaker-than-expected ASP and utilisation ramp.
• Normalisation of Middle East EG supply
• Earnings volatility at Nan Ya Technology and Formosa
Petrochemical.
Company Description
Nan Ya Plastics is a core member of the Formosa Plastics
Group. The company operates four business lines, including

plastics, chemicals, polyester, and electronic materials,
across Taiwan, China, and the US. Nan Ya also holds equity-
method stakes in Nan Ya Technology (2408 TT) and Formosa
Petrochemical (6505 TT), and consolidates PCB exposure
through Nan Ya PCB (8046 TT).

Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a reasonably positive
view on Nan Ya Plastics. The strongest style exposure is
Price Momentum, indicating this stock has had strong
medium to long term returns which often persist into
the future. The weakest style exposure is Profitability,
indicating this stock is not efficiently converting
investments to earnings; proxied by ratios like ROE or ROA.

Very high – highest risk – Stock should be expected to
move up or down 60–100% in a year – investors should be

aware this stock is highly speculative.
High – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 40–60% in a year – investors should be aware this
stock could be speculative.
Medium – stock should be expected to move up or down
at least 25–40% in a year.
Low – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 15–25% in a year.

stock and should be assumed to adjust proportionately

with volatility risk

adjustments made:
Added back: goodwill amortisation, provision for
catastrophe reserves, IFRS derivatives & hedging, IFRS
impairments & IFRS interest expense
Excluded: non recurring items, asset revals, property revals,
appraisal value uplift, preference dividends & minority
interests

### Page 1

M
Update
Unimicron  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 GM beat MSe/Street; stay
OW
Divestiture of Unimicron Japan (UMJ): Unimicron to divest 100% of UMJ to Meiko
Electronics (6787.T, covered by Sota Harashima). According to Meiko’s filing (link),
the share purchase agreement is targeted for December 2026 and the business
handover for April 2027. The price has not been disclosed. UMJ is a niche specialty
PCB maker with no IC substrate exposure. In F3/26, it reported revenue of JPY 8.0B,

an operating loss of JPY 594M, and a loss per share of JPY 16,628.
Capex: Unimicron announced an additional NT$50.9B investment in long-lead-time
equipment, raising the total to ~NT$82.6B, spanning beyond 2027.
Our view: We view the results as modestly negative, as buy-side investors are likely
to place greater emphasis on GM than on non-operating performance. That said, this
appears to reflect an expectations mismatch rather than any structural concern,

with GM continuing to expand meaningfully, up 400bps q/q. We continue to like all
ABF substrate names in our coverage, but prefer Unimicron given its more proactive
expansion plans over the next ~5 years, which should help it gain share from
competitors.

Key Takeaways
3Q GM at 28.8% (+400bps q/q) is 120bps/150bps above MSe/Street, but slightly
below buy-side expectation of ~30%.

3Q non-op gain of ~NT$2.6B is above MSe/Street, and above buy-side
expectation of non-op losses, leading to NP of ~NT$10.1B or ~NT$6.41 EPS.
We view the divestiture of UMJ positively, in-line with Unimicron's long-term
strategy to focus on higher-end, more profitable businesses.
The stock trades at ~14x 2028 P/E, undemanding versus its 5-year average of
~21x.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.

Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers
n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶 (晶圓代工廠 )已確定之 ASIC 合作案，其龍潭新廠無塵室
與基礎設施建置，以及子公司全智科配合美系客戶擴產。 1Q26
至 3Q26 期間取得不動產、廠房及設備 (資本支出 )累計達 102.74
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元
(YoY+267.45%) 。3Q26 已進入量產，下一階段擴產將自 2Q27 啟
動，規劃自 2Q27 起每季開出一層全新無塵室加入量產，後續樓
層無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。預估欣
銓 2027 年稅後 EPS 13.20 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最
重要的增長引擎。而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽
光子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向
上，因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與進場裝
機後，於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓營收成長，
3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。營收依應用別分析，通
訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 / 安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 10.9% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.7%；Storage 佔 9.5%；PC/Consumers 佔 6.6%；
Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他 1.8% 。毛利率的部份， 3Q26 毛利率
45.43% ，增加 5.27 個百分點。 3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元。  
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元：受到 3Q26 營收基期墊高、歐美
客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季 ， 以及手機與通訊
終端產品季節性調節，營收僅小幅成長，毛利率展望方面，由
於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年擴產招募
人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將
略低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持高檔水準；公司努力
進行成本管控和改善產品組合 ， 讓 4Q26 獲利有機會高於 3Q26。
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元。 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.28 元。

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG( 環境、社會、公司治理 )相關政策與行動，並於官方網站
設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目標。  
環境 (Environmental) 
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi( 科學基礎減碳目標倡議 )承諾，強化減碳目標
與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會 (Social) 
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理 (Governance) 
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會 (BSI)ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台灣
企業永續獎 (TCSA) －永續報告獎金獎」 ，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊揭露與
社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議 (SBTi) 官方驗證。  
◼連續  9 年榮獲「公司治理評鑑前  5% 」的殊榮。  
◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。

個股報告

3 群益投顧 
由於全球  AI 需求極其暢旺，許多一線封測同業將產能高度集中在  AI 核
心晶片，導致其他「 AI 周邊晶片」或「非  AI 通訊晶片」的測試產能出現
缺口。欣銓因此獲得大量的外溢訂單 (轉單 )，特別是在通訊與 PC Consumer 
領域。欣銓 3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。  
營收依應用別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9%； 射頻晶片(RF IC) 佔 10.9%； 微處理器(General MCU)
佔 8.7% ； Storage 佔 9.5% ； PC/Consumers 佔 6.6% ； Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他
1.8% 。  
3Q26 終端應用結構，通訊與連網 (Communication/Connectivity) 為最大成長
引擎，營收佔比自 2Q26 的 33.4% 大幅擴張至 41.0% 。這部份含光通訊和晶
圓代工廠的訂單 。 車用與安控(ATV/Security) 營收佔比為 17.9%，略 低 於2Q26
的 18.2% ，不過營收金額是增加，車用市場回溫，營收持續向上。射頻晶片
(RF IC) 營收佔比為 10.9% ，低於 2Q26 的 12.2% ，因為手機拉貨動能下滑。
儲存裝置 (Storage) 營收佔比自 2Q26 的 10.8% 收斂至 9.5% ，因晶圓代工廠的
產能受限。通用型微控制器 (General MCU) 佔比為 8.7% ，相較 2Q26 之 8.5%
微幅增加。個人電腦與消費性晶片 (PC/Consumers) 佔比為 6.6% ，相較 2Q26
之 9.9% 明顯下降，需求受到終端價格上漲影響，加上客戶產品組合上也會
優先供應通訊產品，營收佔比下滑較明顯。記憶體 (Memory) 營收佔比為
3.7% ，低於 2Q26 的 5.4% 。其他類別佔比則由 1.60% 微升至 1.80% 。  
毛利率的部份，得益於通訊連網晶片拉貨動能強勁以及整體稼動率維持高
檔約 80%。營業毛利達 24.25 億元， QoQ+33.1% ， YoY+89.1% ，毛利率攀升
至 45.43% ，較 2Q26 增加 5.27 個百分點，高稼動率帶動規模經濟發酵。營
業利益達 18.6 億元， QoQ+35.0% ，營益率達 34.8% ，較 2Q26 成長 4.4 個百
分點，較 3Q25 大幅增加 10.5 個百分點。業外收支部分， 3Q26 業外淨支出
為 0.34 億元，稅前淨利 18.25 億元， QoQ+25.16% ，稅後 EPS 3.01 元。創下
單季獲利新高紀錄。  
累計前三季 (1Q26 至 3Q26) 合併營收達 138.54 億元， YoY+35.4% ，毛利率達
41.7% ，營業利益達 43.34 億元， YoY+69.60% ，營益率達 31.3% ，累計稅後
淨利為 35.54 億元， YoY+78.4% ，前三季累計 EPS 達 7.49 元。  
就客戶類型分析 ，1Q26 至 3Q26 累計營收中 ， 無晶圓廠IC 設計業者 (Fabless)
貢獻 52.5% ，垂直整合製造商 (IDM) 佔 42.1% ，晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 佔比由
前三季累計的 1.8% 提高至 5.4%，其他客戶佔比為 0.1%。在產品測試型態方
面，前三季晶圓測試 (Wafer Sorting) 營收佔比達 73.2% ，成品測試 (Final Test)
佔比為 26.1% ，其他項目佔 0.7% 。  
基於與策略客戶已談定的  ASIC 合作案，預估欣銓 2026 年資本支出將明
顯增加至約 130 億元， 2027 年將會高於 2026 年：  
欣銓對資本支出一向保持保守與謹慎，主因是高額折舊若遇上稼動率下滑，
會嚴重衝擊毛利率。此次  2026 年轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策略客戶已確
定的  ASIC 合作案。欣銓 2024 至 2025 年的資本支出主要集中在龍潭新廠
的廠房興建與土建尾款； 2026 年則轉向以「機台設備」為主，廠房支出比
例將顯著降低。先進測試機台交期長達 6 - 8 個月。目前測試機台約 1,700
台以上。  
欣銓在 1Q26 至 3Q26 期間取得不動產 、 廠房及設備(資本支出 )累計達 102.74

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 28 日

4 群益投顧 
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元， YoY+267.45% ，
資本支出顯著放量主要由兩大核心動能驅動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室與基
礎設施建置，第二為子公司全智科技為配合美系通訊大客戶強勁 AI 晶片測
試需求而積極採購高階測試設備。 4Q26 因實際投入量產的機台數量相對有
限，單季資本支出將較 3Q26 持平或微幅縮減，預估 2026 年資本支出約 130
億元。但 2027 年資本支出規模掌握度高且明確將超越 2026 年。主因龍潭新
廠單一樓層無塵室與機台設備支出即達 40 億元以上，公司規劃自 2Q27 起
每季開出 1 層全新無塵室加入量產， 2027 年約有 3 個樓層陸續投產，且後
續樓層的無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。  
截至 3Q26 底，公司不動產、廠房及設備帳面價值擴張至 308.94 億元，佔總
資產比重達 64% ，資產總計同步推升至 485.56 億元。  
為支應擴產資金需求，公司除於 1Q26 至 3Q26 透過銀行借款淨增 61.43 億
元外，子公司於 09/2026 處分欣銓股票取得約 40 億元流動資金，後續將以
股利形式匯回母公司以支應資本支出。為因應 2027 年更為龐大的資本支出
銓現階段仍以銀行借款為優先籌資工具，預期負債比率將自 3Q26 底的 50%
進一步拉升，但公司承諾將整體負債比率嚴格控制在 60% 以內，並視擴產階
段適時評估其他籌資工具；同時因擴建資金留存考量，現金股利配發率預計
將由 2026 年的 70% 常態區間下修至 2027 年的 50% 以上。  
欣銓在海外生產基地的布局策略上，維持專注鞏固台灣、新加坡及中國南京
等現有廠區的營運效率與產能發揮。管理層明確指出，赴新國家或地區設立
生產基地必須具備足夠的在地客戶基礎與充沛的訂單量支撐，才能避免新
廠陷入長期營運虧損；基於當前尚未取得足額訂單承諾的考量，欣銓目前並
無赴美國投資建廠的計畫。  
在現有海外據點的產品與客戶定位方面，高階 AI ASIC 與先進製程相關的
測試訂單全數集中於台灣本地廠區，包括龍潭廠及全智科湖口廠。新加坡廠
則以承接成熟產品線為主，產品結構涵蓋車用、工控及消費性晶片，主要原
因在於新加坡當地目前尚無大量先進晶圓代工產能；後續欣銓在新加坡廠
區的成長策略，將聚焦於進一步深化與聯電 (UMC) 及世界先進 (VIS) 在新加
坡當地晶圓廠的委外測試合作機會。  
折舊費用方面，欣銓已公告自 1/1/2025 起，部分機器設備耐用年限由 7 年
延長至 9 年，這將有助於反映資產實際經濟效益，欣銓 2025 年折舊費用
31.39 億元。龍潭廠量產機台之折舊費用已有部分於 3Q26 開始提列，但因
部分設備驗收時點落於季中之後，折舊並未全額反映在 3Q26 單季，預期
4Q26 折舊費用將進一步高於 3Q26 ，預估 2026 年折舊費用約 35 億元。進入
2027 年後，新設備折舊將隨各季機台進場與驗收進度按部就班提列；依產
業慣例，新機台通常需於投產前一季進場進行裝機與驗證，例如 2Q27 投產
之產能將於 1Q27 進場裝機，驗收完成後即開始提列折舊。儘管折舊費用持
續墊高且 4Q26 至 1Q27 提前招募人力與產能整備將使毛利率自 3Q26 的
45.43% 高點略為回落，但因龍潭廠 4Q26 產能利用率維持滿載、全智湖口新
廠產能將於 2027 年達全產全銷，產能高稼動率預期可有效稀釋固定折舊成
本，使單季毛利率持續穩健維持在 40% 以上。  
產能利用率已達滿載，預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前
淨利 18.57 億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元：  
展望 4Q26 營運，欣銓預估合併營收將呈現 QoQ+3% 左右，主要受到 3Q26

5 群益投顧 
營收基期墊高、歐美客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季，以及手
機與通訊終端產品季節性調節所致。  
旗下全智總坪數逾 3,500 坪的湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光子相關新
產品的量產階段，預期 4Q26 可以開出 20-25% 的產能，全智湖口廠客戶需
求高，因此 2027 年可以達到滿載狀況。  
毛利率展望方面，由於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年
擴產招募人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將略
低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持在高檔水準。定價策略方面，欣銓對
主力客戶價格維持穩定，但已針對低毛利與零星客戶提出調價，部分客戶已
順利反映漲價。公司努力進行成本管控和改善產品組合，讓 4Q26 獲利有機
會高於 3Q26。預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前淨利 18.57
億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元。  
預估欣銓 2026 年營收 193.78 億元， YoY+38.13% ，稅前淨利 63.05 億元，
YoY+74.58% ，稅後 EPS 10.28 元。  
欣銓提供完整的光電測試 (全智 )與先進異質整合封裝 (瑞峰 )解決方案，建構
完整 CPO 生態系：  
欣銓集團在矽光子領域的布局完整，透過結合母公司與旗下轉投資公司的
資源，建構完整 CPO( 共封裝光學 )生態系，欣銓與全智皆為  SEMI 矽光子
產業聯盟及異質整合矽光子產業聯盟的重要成員。欣銓已布局光通訊與矽
光子 (CPO) 高階測試架構，目前接洽並配合之客戶達三家以上，測試製程至
少已切入至 Insertion 1 和 3 階段，並持續往 Insertion 4 進行技術評估與了
解。由於目前全球矽光子晶片出貨量仍處於技術開發與初期工程驗證階段，
市場尚未進入大規模營收爆發期，因此公司尚未設定具體之 CPO 營收貢獻
目標，但已透過全智湖口廠與美系通訊核心客戶建立緊密夥伴關係，提早卡
位高速傳輸光電整合封裝之長期測試商機。  
欣銓集團的矽光子佈局中，全智科技 (欣銓 100% 轉投資 )主要負責  EIC 測
試、PIC 測試以及光電整合測試 。 其中 EIC 測試已進入量產多年 ， 並於 2024 
年建置矽光子專用的光電整合測試實驗室，提供全方位的工程測試與驗證
服務。瑞峰半導體 (欣銓投資約  25%) 專注於先進異質整合封裝 (如 CPO 3D 
TSV/CoW ， EIC+PIC) 。瑞峰負責將 EIC 晶圓疊加在 PIC 晶圓上 (Chip on 
Wafer) ，隨後再交由全智進行光電整合測試。 Fabrinet( 策略投資夥伴 )主要負
責後續的  CPO 相關製程與光學引擎等項目。  
全智 (湖口新廠 )3Q26 正式量產，總面積超過  3,500 坪的湖口新廠，將著重
於矽光子測試。根據客戶端訊息，該廠區矽光子新產品的產能利用率拉升速
度預期將會相當快，且已與客戶洽談，有機會在  2027 年接近滿載。在  CPO 
的  EIC/PIC 測試產能上，目前已佈建超過  150 台設備。中長期擴廠計畫，
為因應強勁的光通訊客戶需求，湖口廠目前正在整理無塵室空間，希望能再
騰出 1~2 層樓來配合擴充；同時，為 2028 年以後的長期成長，公司也已準
備著手尋求購買新土地。  
瑞峰已於  2024 年建置第一條  12 吋矽光子晶圓級封裝 (WLP) 生產線。目
前將  EIC 疊在  PIC 晶圓上的  Chip on Wafer 製程正逐漸進入量產。 2025 
年已導入數家新客戶，進行超過  30 項  NPI( 新產品導入 )驗證； 2026 年更

6 群益投顧 
預計會有超過  10 家新客戶 (Tape-out / New design) 進行導入與設計中。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元：  
展望 2027 年營運前景，欣銓管理層對整體營收增長維持正面樂觀基調，主
要受惠於 2 大核心擴產引擎全面啟動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室自 2Q27 起進
入逐季開出階段，第二為子公司全智科技新竹湖口新廠產能將有機會達到
全產全銷。  
除晶圓代工廠外包訂單外，美系通訊核心客戶對高階 AI 晶片測試需求自
4Q26 一路強勁延伸至 2027 全年，全智湖口新廠產能已獲客戶全數包下，若
產能利用率達到滿載運作下，預期有機會貢獻 2027 年單月營收 1~1.5 億元。
車用與安控 (ATV/Security) 及儲存裝置 (Storage) 受惠於 Edge AI 與資料中心
需求回溫， 2027 年營收貢獻將持續擴張。  
季度走勢上，延續 4Q26 營收 QoQ+1~3% 的走勢， 1Q27 受到農曆春節工作
天數減少與季節性影響，預估營收將持平於 4Q26 ，毛利率受前期準備費用
影響維持在 40% 以上高檔水準，整體產能利用率 (UTR) 則維持約 80% 健康水
位。真正的成長將自 2Q27 啟動，欣銓龍潭廠共有 7 層無塵室，第 1 層已於
2Q26 年完成認證，自 07/2026 正式開始貢獻營收與量產。第 2 至第 3 層無
塵室工程預計於 2026 年底至 1H27 完工，其中第 2 層產能最快自 2Q27 開始
貢獻營運，依每季啟用一層的節奏逐步推進。  
獲利能力與定價方面，雖然新廠開出初期伴隨折舊與人力整備等成本，但隨
著 AI 測試需求暢旺帶動整體產能利用率持續站穩高檔，加上高階晶圓測試
比重維持高檔，評估 2027 年毛利率將高於 2026 年。此外，公司針對主要長
期配合之大型客戶維持穩定報價，但對於低毛利與零星客戶已啟動調價提
議並陸續反映漲價，進一步強化整體產品組合獲利體質。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元。

  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Document 169: 63亿美元！币安遭“史上最大”黑客洗劫，暂停全部存储和提取功能，承诺将承担所有损失

Source PDF: all report pdf/63亿美元！币安遭“史上最大”黑客洗劫，暂停全部存储和提取功能，承诺将承担所有损失.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5

### Page 1

币安创始⼈赵⻓鹏已公开确认攻击属实，但强调⽤⼾资⾦安全。币安最新宣布，估计总影
响⾦额约为 63 亿美元。 TRM 全球政策负责⼈ Ari Redbord 称其为 “ ⼈类历史上最⼤的银⾏抢
劫案 ” 。值得注意的是，此次事件发⽣于 " ⿊⾊⼗⽉ " ⽹络攻击浪潮期间， AI 技术催⽣新型攻
击⼿段被认为是重要背景因素。
摘要：
全球最⼤加密货币交易所币安周四遭遇史上规模最⼤的加密货币⿊客攻击，被迫暂停全平台存
取款业务，事件震动整个加密市场。此次⿊客攻击造成的损失约达 63 亿美元，远超此前任何⼀
起同类事件。币安称，估计总影响⾦额约为 63 亿美元。
据彭博最先报道，此次⼤规模资⾦异动引发市场⾼度警觉。币安创始⼈赵⻓鹏 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇在社交
平台 X 上发⽂确认， “ 看起来确实是针对币安的⿊客攻击 ”，但同时强调⽤⼾资⾦安全。稳定币发
⾏商 Tether 和 Circle 随即采取紧急⾏动，合计冻结逾 15 亿美元与此次事件相关的资产，以限制潜
在损失蔓延。币安最新宣布，经核实，冻结资⾦总额约 30 亿美元。

币安随后在 X 平台发⽂宣布，将承担所有损失，并已暂停全部存储和提取功能，同时与执法机关
展开合作，承诺调查完成后将公布完整的事后调查报告。若最终确认，此次事件将打破 2025 年
朝鲜⿊客从 Bybit 盗取 15 亿美元所创下的加密货币史上最⼤劫案纪录。

### Page 2

史上最⼤⽹络劫案
此次针对币安的攻击规模史⽆前例。华尔街⽇报称， TRM 全球政策负责⼈ Ari Redbord 称其为 “ ⼈
类历史上最⼤的银⾏抢劫案 ” 。
据币安在 X 平台发表的声明，初步调查显⽰，此次攻击涉及 “ ⼀名 DevOps ⼯程师的笔记本电脑
遭到⼊侵 ” ，攻击者借此得以 " 提交未经授权的提款交易 " 。
据华尔街⽇报，这⼀攻击路径揭⽰了加密货币交易所⾯临的结构性脆弱性。与证券交易所不
同，许多加密货币交易所直接代客⼾托管资产，将⼤量资⾦集中存放于加密钱包中，使其成为
⿊客的⾼价值⽬标。
在币安公布 63 亿美元的⾦额之前，华尔街⽇报报道指出，区块链安全机构 SEAL 911 的 Taylor
Monahan 表⽰，攻击者实际获利“ 远超 20 亿美元 ” ，最⾼可能达 55 亿美元。 “ 信息变化很快，确认
确切数字还需要时间， ”Monahan 说。华尔街⽇报联系的其他多位⽹络安全专家则将数字定在 55
亿美元以上。
据 Axios 报道，密切追踪加密市场的分析师和交易员估计，此次疑似⽹络攻击中被盗⾦额介于 17

亿⾄ 57 亿美元之间。

### Page 3

Tether ⾸席执⾏官 Paolo Ardoino 在 X 上发⽂称，该公司已冻结逾 15.67 亿美元的数字货币，原因
是 “ 与币安交易所潜在⿊客攻击相关 ” 。 Circle ⾸席执⾏官 Jeremy Allaire 则表⽰， Circle 已配合执
法部⻔冻结逾 8 亿美元 USDC，并将持续⽀持调查。
报道称， Tether 和 Circle 的快速介⼊，在⼀定程度上遏制了损失进⼀步蔓延。赵⻓鹏最初援引的
被冻结资产规模为约 17 亿美元，币安随后将这⼀数字修正为跨多类资产共计约 30 亿美元。
稳定币发⾏⽅冻结资产的机制是：将⿊客的加密钱包地址列⼊⿊名单，使被盗代币⽆法移动、
转账或套现，从⽽在⿊客将其兑换为其他加密货币或通过混币器洗钱之前将资⾦锁定。
币安回应：⽤⼾资⾦安全，将承担全部损失
⾯对外界关切，币安⽅⾯给出了明确表态。
币安发⾔⼈在声明中表⽰： “ 我们的团队已知悉有关潜在安全问题的报告，正在积极调查。请放
⼼，⽤⼾资⾦完全安全。 “该发⾔⼈还表⽰，安全团队正在 “ 紧急审查所有相关信息 ” ，并提醒⽤

⼾ “ 对诈骗保持警惕 “ 。
币安随后在 X 平台进⼀步宣布，公司将承担所有损失，⽬前正不间断处理此事，⼒求解决问题，
并在安全检查期间暂停了所有存储和提取功能，同时与执法机关保持合作。调查完成后，币安
承诺将公布完整的事后调查报告。
赵⻓鹏虽已于 2023 年卸任币安⾸席执⾏官，⽬前仍为公司最⼤股东。他在 X 上写道： “ 作为股
东，我预期所有⽤⼾资⾦将得到全额覆盖。 ” 、

⽬前，币安尚未确认攻击者⾝份，失窃资产的最终规模也有待进⼀步核实。
规模虽⼤，币安抗压能⼒相对较强
尽管事件规模空前，市场分析⼈⼠认为币安凭借⾃⾝体量，具备相对较强的抗冲击能⼒。
加密货币交易公司 JPEG Trading 联合创始⼈ Kevin March 表⽰，“ 以绝对美元⾦额⽽⾔，这是⼀
次巨⼤的打击 ”，但考虑到币安拥有 12 亿美元的保险基⾦，加之 Tether 和 Circle 迅速冻结被盗资
产，预计损失在可控范围之内。
“ 如果这件事发⽣在加密⾏业任何其他公司⾝上，将是灾难性的， ”March 说。他指出，币安的规
模使其具备更强的隔离能⼒，可通过保险和资产负债表覆盖潜在损失，但加密市场价格的⼴泛
波动仍有可能发⽣。
值得关注的是，与传统股票交易所不同，加密货币交易所通常直接为⽤⼾托管数字资产，这使

其天然成为⿊客的⾼价值攻击⽬标。此外，存放在币安等平台的资⾦并不受政府存款保险保
障，任何赔付均取决于交易所⾃⾝的意愿和能⼒。
此次事件并⾮孤⽴发⽣，⽽是近期⼀系列⾼级别⽹络攻击浪潮的组成部分。
据 Axios 报道，此次疑似⿊客攻击发⽣于所谓“ ⿊⾊⼗⽉ ”期间 —— ⼀系列⿊客攻击和 AI 发现的漏
洞接连触发，引发外界对全球性⽹络安全危机的担忧。此次币安事件发⽣的数⼩时前，去中⼼
化预测市场平台 Polymarket 刚刚确认遭遇⽹络攻击，被迫暂停交易约⼀⼩时，攻击者⾄少卷⾛
270 万美元⽤⼾资⾦。
安全研究机构 Hudson Rock 将 Polymarket 事件与第三⽅⽹站监控⼯具 DebugBear 的数据泄露相
关联，后者当天早些时候已宣布正在应对⾃⾝的安全事故。
NBC 新闻指出，此次币安⼤规模资⾦异动发⽣之际，全球⽹络安全防线正处于⾼度戒备状态，
原因正是近期⼈⼯智能技术的快速进步催⽣了新型攻击⼿段的出现。

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 
SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수
주 확대 본격화

minho.hur@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 85,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
56,700 
유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

KOSPI 5937.66 
시가총액 36,320십억원

표 2. 웨스팅하우스의 미국 및 글로벌 신규 원전 프로젝트 현황 (초기 사업발굴 단계 제외)  
 프로젝트·사업 기수 발전용량 Cameco 분류 단계 및 비고 
미국 DOE 공급망 금융지원 사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 장납기 품목 조달 금융지원, 7개 유틸리티와 논의 중 
 미 상무부 전략적 협력사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 정부 협력사업(한국은 6기 투자/건설) 
 V.C. Summer 2·3호기 공사 재개 2기 2.3GW 장납기 품목 발주·EPC(LLI/EPC) 
폴란드 Lubiatowo-Kopalino 3기 3.5GW 장납기 품목 발주·EPC(LLI/EPC) 
불가리아 Kozloduy 7·8호기 2기 2.3GW 초기 서비스 계약(ESC) 
우크라이나 Khmelnytskyi 5·6호기 2기 2.3GW 초기 서비스 계약(ESC) 
정부 지원·협력 및 계약 기반 사업 소계  최대 29기 최대 33.4GW FEED·초기 사업발굴 제외 
FEED 사업 네덜란드, 슬로베니아, 핀란드, 스웨덴, 미국 합계 11기 합계 12.7GW 기본설계(FEED)

표 3. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 
항목 공식 발표 내용 
조건부 대출 약정 규모 최대 175억 달러·US$17.5 billion 
지원 대상 최대 5개 프로젝트, 프로젝트당 AP1000 2기, 합계 10기 
프로젝트 소유 구조 Westinghouse와 해당 전력회사 또는 에너지기업이 공동 소유 
대출 접근 전 자기자본 요건 프로젝트당 Westinghouse 5억 달러, 상대 전력회사·에너지기업 5억 달러, 합계 10억 달러를 사전에 확약 
조달 방식 Westinghouse가 참여 기업과 함께 장납기품목을 고정가격으로 조달 
조달 시점 자기자본 확약 시점과 기타 조건에 따라 프로젝트별 구매를 순차적으로 진행 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

Document 245: AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates
Date:    1 day ago
AI Jitters Continue While Fed Pauses Hiking Rates

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
AI Jitters Continue While Fed
Pauses Hiking Rates
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 29 Oct 2026
View in browser

I. The AI Boom Versus The AI Trade
AI jitters will be back tomorrow after taking a breather so far this week, as
encouraging developments surrounding the AI investment boom helped
restore confidence and lift technology stocks. The S&P 500 rose 0.3% on
Wednesday, moving back above its 50-day moving average (chart).

### Page 2

The Nasdaq-100 advanced 0.6%, recovering toward its 50-day moving
average while remaining above its 200-day moving average (chart).
Among chipmakers, Micron jumped 5.9%, AMD gained 3.2%, and
Broadcom rose 2.8% (chart). SK Hynix shares surged as much as 9.4% in
Seoul following blockbuster earnings.
Three recent developments helped revive confidence in the AI trade:
(1) More spending. $235 billion in prospective AI financing emerged this
week, including Anthropic's expected $100 billion IPO raise, Meta's plans

to raise up to $95 billion through equity issuance, and SpaceX's $40 billion
debt deal to purchase GPUs. These developments suggest capital
remains available for the AI buildout, with equity markets providing an
alternative if debt financing tightens.

### Page 3

(2) Encouraging AI economics. Anthropic's S-1 filing offered five
encouraging signals about the sustainability of compute demand. First, H1
revenues surged from $1.2 billion to $16.3 billion y/y. Second, operating
losses narrowed from $2.1 billion to $693 million, with Q2 marking its first
quarterly operating profit. Third, 11,900 customers now spend at least
$100,000 annually, up from roughly 950 a year earlier. Fourth, revenue
per dollar of compute expense increased more than fivefold to $2.08.

Fifth, Anthropic held $131.7 billion in cash and short-term investments to
finance expansion.
(3) Strong semiconductor earnings. SK Hynix's Q3 results reinforced
confidence in AI infrastructure demand. Operating profit surged more than
sevenfold y/y, while the company announced roughly $75 billion in
shareholder returns. Most encouragingly, management expects memory
shortages through 2030, with production effectively sold out through 2029,
suggesting durable demand and pricing power.

These developments reinforce our view that the AI investment boom is
here to stay. But that doesn't mean that the AI trade won't remain jittery.
This evening, Alphabet shares are down about 10% after the company
missed Q3 consensus revenues and earnings estimates. Free cash flow
was also more negative than expected, while the company remains
committed to significant capex of over $200 billion this year and $300
billion in 2027. Long-term debt rose to $125.1 billion, up from $21.6 billion

a year earlier.
There was some good news. Cloud revenue more than doubled to $30.7
billion. Google said its Gemini app reached 1 billion users, making it the
fastest-growing product in its history. Furthermore, Microsoft shares rose
4% in extended trading this evening after the company reported better-
than-expected quarterly results, driven by 48% growth in its Azure cloud
infrastructure business.
II. The Fed Pauses Before Midterm Elections
The FOMC voted 10-2 Wednesday to hold the federal funds rate at

3.75%-4.00%, with Lisa Cook and Beth Hammack favoring a 25bps hike.
Chair Kevin Warsh acknowledged robust economic growth, a healthy
labor market, stubborn inflation, and financial conditions that aren't tight.
Yet he cited tightening financial conditions, anchored inflation

### Page 4

expectations, and improving productivity as reasons for patience. Here's
more:
(1) Economic activity. Warsh observed that "economic growth has
strengthened." Consumer spending remains robust and business
investment is growing at a double-digit pace. The capex story, he said, is
"not just robust, not just strong, but it is broadening."
(2) Labor market. Unemployment is lower than a year ago, payroll growth
has improved, and initial jobless claims remain near historic lows.

Employment conditions are "quite positive," Warsh said, with FOMC
discussions focused primarily on price stability.
(3) Inflation. "Inflation is too high," Warsh acknowledged, warning that
rising energy prices could spread through the economy. He rejected a
higher implicit inflation target: "There's no soft inflation target, there's no
implicit inflation target." Still, he believes inflation expectations remain
anchored.
(4) Financial conditions. Warsh noted that tighter financial conditions are

prompting more selective capital allocation. Rejecting claims that AI
investment is insensitive to interest rates, he observed that "interest rates
get in the cracks" as credit spreads have widened for some borrowers.
Nevertheless, "while overall financial conditions are tighter, I'm unwilling
and unable to call them tight."
(5) Productivity. Warsh disputed that robust growth necessarily implies
overheating: "That isn't the judgment of most of my colleagues." Improving

productivity, much of it predating recent AI advances, may have raised
potential growth without fueling inflation.
(6) Revamping the Fed. Warsh reiterated his opposition to forward
guidance outside crises. Yet other officials' comments shifted market
expectations away from an October hike despite hotter-than-expected
September CPI and PPI inflation reports, suggesting forward guidance
remains intact. He also plans to dedicate one FOMC meeting in 2027 and

two in 2028 to monetary policy strategy.
In our view, stronger productivity growth will let the economy expand
faster without adding inflationary pressure. That could be an argument

### Page 5

against restrictive monetary policy, but not against returning to neutral,
which we estimate at 4.25%-4.50%. We agree that September's hike
“removed a dose of accommodation,” but believe another 50bps is
needed to remove the rest. The Fed doesn't need to throw sand in the
economy's gears. It just needs to take its foot off the accelerator. We
wouldn't rule out a 50bps hike in December.
💡

clause.

Document 270: Capital Market Outlook (2026-09-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-09-28).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The Five Shocks of 2026—And the Extraordinary Resilience of the U.S. Economy
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
This is the year the consensus got mugged.
Rewind to the start of the year. The market consensus was that the U.S. economy was
set to slow. The Fed was set to cut interest rates. Corporate earnings were set to grow—

but not explode. Energy prices would remain tame. And the odds looked good that the
Republicans would control the House, the Senate, or both, following the midterm election.
Nine months on, the landscape looks starkly different. Defined by five shocks—energy,
interest rates, earnings, growth, and politics—2026 looks nothing like the year investors
thought they were buying on January 1.
Shock 1: Diesel Matters. While global oil prices have moved higher since the start of

the U.S./Iran war on February 28, oil prices have yet to surpass their previous record high
because the world has released some 400 million barrels from strategic reserves this year,
China has curbed its demand for oil imports, and the U.S. has goosed its energy exports to
record highs.
But here is the problem: While gasoline powers the American consumer, diesel powers the
American economy—and diesel prices are now sitting at record highs. Diesel supplies have

been hammered by wars in the Middle East and Ukraine that have sharply reduced refining
capacity. According to the International Energy Agency, by August, exports of diesel from
refiners in the Middle East had fallen by a quarter from prewar levels. Meanwhile, Ukraine
has hit 24 of Russia’s 34 largest refineries, reducing supplies from one of the world’s
largest diesel exporters. The upshot: U.S. diesel prices have soared from $3.55 a gallon
at the start of the year to roughly $6.52 a gallon. While crude oil trades for around $100

a barrel, the retail equivalent of a barrel of diesel now costs more than $260. The diesel
shock of 2026 was the last thing investors saw coming—and remains a key impediment to
structurally lower inflation.
Shock 2: From Interest Rate Cuts to Rate Hikes. Perhaps no other consensus view
entering 2026 has been turned more dramatically on its head than expectations for the
Fed. Heading into 2026, markets were pricing in rate cuts on expectations of a cooling

labor market, moderating inflation, and slower economic growth. The Fed easing cycle was
expected to continue in 2026. In January 2026, futures showed a roughly 50% chance of a
cut by March.
Expectations, however, flipped in late February with the start of the U.S./Iran war. The
Middle East energy shock reignited inflationary pressures, which, combined with stronger-
than-expected U.S. economic growth and a tightening labor market, have demolished the

odds of easier money in 2026. Short- and long-term Treasury yields rose sharply last week
in the wake of the S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI)
which pointed to the strongest business expansion in more than five years and the fastest
employment growth in over four years, while also reinforcing expectations that the Fed
will maintain a restrictive policy stance for longer. Markets were expecting two to three
quarter-point rate cuts in 2026; instead, the Fed has already raised rates once this year and

is expected to keep raising rates over the near term.
Shock 3: The Earnings Boom No One Saw Coming. Not all the shocks of 2026 are
negative. Indeed, stronger-than-expected corporate earnings have been among the most
positive factors of this year. Corporate America entered the year facing questions about
tariffs, AI-related capital expenditure spending, the health of the U.S. consumer, operating
leverage and the U.S. dollar outlook. At the start of the year, Wall Street was expecting

roughly $290 of S&P 500 earnings per share for 2026, per Bloomberg estimates. But by
September 21, the estimate had risen by $50, to $340 per share.
Investment Implications
While some of these shocks may
contribute to periods of market
volatility, the combination of strong
earnings growth, robust domestic
demand, and ongoing capital
investment suggests that the
fundamental backdrop for Equities
remains constructive.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Earnings have been upgraded and underpinned by several factors, including robust final 
domestic demand in the U.S., a pickup in global growth, AI-related capital spending, 
productivity gains, and enhanced operating leverage. All these variables have helped deliver 
an earnings “shock” to Wall Street. And as an aside, yes, headline profit growth has been 
inflated by strong technology earnings and one-off investment gains from Amazon and 
Alphabet. That said, the S&P 500’s earnings story remains extraordinary. According to 
FactSet, the 493 companies excluding the Magnificent 7 1 
posted 31.8% earnings growth in 
Q2, the strongest level since late 2021.

Shock 4: An Economy with more Horsepower than the Headlines Suggest. That 
corporate profits have exceeded expectations this ye
ar is due in no small part to an economy 
that continues to surprise to the upside. The Fed was supposed to cut rates this year to 
keep the economy humming and prevent it from losing steam. But rather than limp through 
2026, the economy has leaped ahead. Yes, real gross domestic product (GDP) growth of 
2.1% in Q1 and 1.5% in Q2 are hardly blockbuster annualized rates of growth. However, the 
figures belie the underlying strength of the U.S. economy, with real final domestic demand

(which strips out trade) running at a 4.2% annual rate in Q2. According to the latest reading 
of the Atlanta Fed GDP tracker, Q3 real GDP grow
th is running at a red-hot rate of 5.1%. 
Slowdown? What slowdown?
Resilient consumer spending, robust capital investment, record leve
ls of new business 
formation, and strong foreign direct investment inflows have combined to stoke growth 
in the $32 trillion behemoth called the U.S. economy—the most resilient and dynamic 
economy in the world.
Shock 5: The Unexpected Washington Reset. The last shock is still in the making, but 
given current trends, the odds are rising that the Democrats could control both the House 
and Senate follow
ing the midterm elections. To this point, in January, and per Kalshi, the 
Democrats had a 71% shot at the House and an 18% chance of taking the Senate. Fast-
forward to today, and the odds are a 90% chance that the Democrats take the House and a 
60% chance that they take the Senate.
How all of this plays out remains to be seen—there are some 36 days before the November 
vote, and anything can happen. The market base case remains that the Democrats take the 
House, and the Senate is a toss-up—or too close to call. But with the Democrats expected to 
control at least one chamber of Congress, inv
estors can expect more volatility and uncertainty 
about negotiations ov
er the budget, debt ceiling, defense spending and AI regulation. Another 
political wildcard to watch: the changing dynamics within the Democratic Party.
The Biggest Shock of All for 2026? Pe
rhaps it’s not $6 diesel, rising interest rates, 
solid growth, booming profits or shifting politics. Rather, it’s the ability of the U.S. 
economy to absorb the unexpected, not buckle under stress, and continue generating  wealth 
for investors. To wit, the S&P 500 is on pace for its fourth
 consecutive year of double-digit 
returns.

MARKET VIEW
2026 Has, So Far, Defied the Traditional Midterm Year Playbook
Jordy Fuentes, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Current Landscape. To no surprise, market volatility persisted in 2026. Performance initially
tracked historical midterm election patterns before diverging meaningfully as the year
progressed, though there are still three months to go. Several items remain at hand as U.S.

voters head to the polls in November, including geopolitical and foreign policy uncertainty,
energy driven inflation pressures, affordability concerns, U.S. fiscal trajectory, and rapid
advancements in AI. Regardless of the eventual political outcome, history suggests that
markets and the economy can perform well across different configurations of government.
In our view, markets continue to be anchored by strong fundamentals and encouraging
seasonal trends.

To Be Addressed. U.S. domestic and foreign policy remain in focus as the global reorder,
tit-for-tat tariffs, and geopolitical conflicts spanning the Middle East to Eastern Europe have
added layers of uncertainty to the U.S. economic outlook.
At the same time, persistent geopolitical flare-ups have pushed oil prices materially
higher, adding to inflation pressures and keeping them above the Fed’s 2% target.
Headline Consumer Price Index (CPI) held above 3% year-over-year (YoY) in August, while

its core measure, which excludes food and energy, held at 2.4% YoY. The latest Personal
Consumption Expenditure (PCE), the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation, also remained
above 3% YoY for both its headline and core figures. 2
The U.S. fiscal trajectory is another area of focus. The U.S. budget deficit stands at 5.4% of
GDP , just above this century’s average of 4.6%. Meanwhile, U.S. public sector debt surpassed
the $40 trillion mark for the first time in August, oscillating around 100% of GDP .3 While these

trends warrant close monitoring, we view them as a slow-moving structural headwind rather
than an immediate concern, particularly within a highly innovative and diverse U.S. economy.
AI has emerged as a policy consideration, with debates increasingly centered on both
regulatory safeguards and the economic implications of the expanding infrastructure buildout.
For voters, those concerns extend to affordability, as the significant energy requirements of

new data centers raise questions about potential pressure on electricity costs.
Strap In for The Ride. Leading up to the midterm election, markets tend to experience
greater swings as investors navigate uncertainty surrounding the policy path for the
remainder of the presidential cycle (Exhibit 2A). Since 1930, the S&P 500 has averaged an
intrayear drawdown of 20% in midterm years, with the latest election in 2022 experiencing
a 25% intrayear pullback. This year, however, the S&P 500 largest peak-to-trough drawdown

has been 9%, considerably shallower than its long-term average (Exhibit 2B). Yet while
volatility may rise as election day approaches, 2026 has increasingly defied the traditional
midterm year playbook.
Why 2026 is Different. Despite entering a challenging stretch of the presidential cycle,
U.S. Equities have materially outpaced the typical year two pattern (Exhibit 2C). The S&P
500 experienced its best Q2 in a midterm year since 1938, surging 14.9% compared to an

average decline of 1.6%. From a monthly perspective, September stands out as the weakest
month, though month-to-date performance points to an unusual 1.0% rise. That resilience
follows a 2.6% gain in August, another month in which performance meaningfully exceeded
the historical decline of 0.5% (Exhibit 2D).
Through September, U.S. Equity resilience has also extended beyond headline returns with
both the frequency and magnitude of market pullbacks remaining relatively subdued. Market

corrections of 10% or more have occurred in just 29% of midterm election years since 1930.4
Against this backdrop, the market’s last correction came in spring 2025, while this year has
2 Bloomberg, Bureau of Labor Statistics. Data through August 31, 2026.
3 U.S. Treasury, Bureau of Economic Analysis. Data as of September 21, 2026.
4 Bloomberg, data through December 31, 2022.
Investment Implications
In our view, any related market
weakness could represent a

potential buying opportunity for
long-term investors.

5 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
seen only one 5%+ pullback compared to the average three per year, underscoring relatively
resilient market conditions.5 Year-to-date, the S&P 500 has increased 13%, well above the
average annual return of 3% according to Bloomberg through September 25, and on pace
to mark the strongest midterm election year since 2006. Taken together, 2026 has thus far
defied the traditionally weak midterm year seasonal pattern, reinforcing that every election

cycle is unique and historical trends may be a useful guide to navigate market volatility.
What’s Ahead? Forward performance is increasingly constructive for Equities. In the
post-war period, the S&P 500 rose 100% of the time in the six and 12 months following
elections, averaging 13% and 14%, respectively. 6 Even narrowing the time frame to three
months, markets ended higher 90% of the time. As investors push through the finish line,
the last three months of the year tend to be the strongest with October standing out,

ending higher 67% of the time. On average, Q4 has gained 5.6% since 1930, far surpassing
the preceding three quarters of election years. 7 Greater clarity about the U.S. fiscal policy
path serves as a potential tailwind for investors following the election.
To Take Home. Volatility tends to rise during midterm years, but history suggests that
reacting to short-term political uncertainty has rarely rewarded long-term investors. Over
longer horizons, the data suggests Equity returns and economic strength are shaped less

by election outcomes than by corporate profits, valuations, labor market conditions and
capital investments.

THE TICKER TAPE
What is the bond market telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
We believe the bond market is telling us that the Fed is on track to meeting their dual
mandate of price stability and full employment. More importantly, when examining the
full yield curve the 2-year Treasury yield is currently around 4.88%, which typically is
the most sensitive to the fed funds rate and tends to price in future potential policy

adjustments. At the same time, the 10-year Treasury yield is currently about 5.11%. This
leads us to believe that the market expects the Fed to engineer a couple of more hikes
while the back end of the curve is indicating that economic growth is still healthy.
Taken together, it appears the full curve, which has clearly flattened, believes the Fed is
doing its job, while supply shocks, for obvious reasons, are not leading to higher long-run
inflation. The backup in long-dated yields is growth, not necessarily inflation as indicated

by rising “real” yields. Therefore, we view the drift higher in yields as a more normal sign
than what many have originally suggested. We continue to emphasize higher economic
growth for longer, rising productivity in the medium term, supply shortages to remain in
key areas within the AI space, and shocks in the energy equation. We expect the energy
shocks to ultimately fade away over time, but we do not believe that growth fades
anytime soon. In our view, a more normal bond market pricing in Fed moves at the short

end, and growth at the long end helps financial conditions and economic growth operate
in a more durable manner.
In summary, the front-end of the curve expects 3 to 4 more hikes in the next 18 months
or so. This can be debated obviously based on future data especially short-term energy
supply shocks. Long-end yields are drifting higher based on five main factors below, in
our view.
1. Strong economic growth (both
services and manufacturing)
2. Hawkish Fed

3. Debt and deficit concerns
4. Lower demand for long duration
sovereign debt in general
5. Energy supply shocks
The largest weight being strong economic activity which kick starts portfolio
repositioning particularly as the Fed hikes rates. The repositioning stops once demand
rises again when yields reach attractive levels for the more patient longer term
investment community. The supply shocks ultimately end and debt and deficit worries will
likely remain, but they still take a backseat for now. Longer duration government debt is

becoming more attractive each week even in the face of the concerns above. Be patient

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500 Index Price Return is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States. A price return index is a stock
market index that tracks only the price changes and capital gains or losses of its component securities, without including dividends or cash distributions.
S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index is an early monthly economic indicator that estimates overall private-sector business activity by combining manufacturing

investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Recent concerns have pushed France’s 10yr spread over Germany’s, to its highest since 2012
and the Euro crisis. But in an historical perspective, today’s spreads are only high by the
standards of the Euro Area when the two shared a common currency. They were regularly
several percentage points apart in the 70s and 80s.

France has also seen its debt rise in relative terms. As recently as the GFC in 2008, France’s
debt-to-GDP was basically in line with Germany’s and well below Italy’s, but today it’s far
above Germany’s and closing in on Italy’s. Outside of the temporary Covid spike, Italian
debt/GDP has been stable for over a decade.

For most of the last decade, nominal GDP has been above 10yr yields, but that has reversed,
which is challenging from a debt sustainability if sustained – especially when debt/GDP is
pushing up against 120%. Recent inflation will slowly push up nominal GDP but the
relationship between the two will be important going forward.

Inflation is always a double-edged sword when it comes to debt. An unexpected burst of
inflation can help lower debt, as we saw right after the pandemic, but it becomes a challenge
when higher yields offset this. This is now the 3rd biggest inflation spike since the Euro Area’s
formation in 1999.

出，費城半導體指數漲勢尤其明顯 。雖然近

官員們認為年底前至少還有一次升息 ，主因
通膨偏高 、經濟與勞動市場保持韌性 ，因此

念股，且日本央行雖升息但並無更鷹派的態
度，日圓走弱並激勵出口類股走勢 。歐股則
缺乏明確方向 ，雖科技股彈升及油價回落提
供支撐 ，然而全球央行緊縮及公債殖利率走
升壓抑估值 ，且法國財政疑慮亦推升風險溢
價，德國因執政聯盟主要政黨在地方選舉遭
遇挫敗，增加了政策與政治上的不確定性。

訊，工業類股亦有不錯表現 ，其餘類股則漲
跌互見。

5
► 隨人工智慧運算需求持續攀升 ，谷歌、亞馬
遜、臉書、微軟及OpenAI等企業巨頭積極發
展客製化晶片 (ASIC)，藉此提升運算效率並
優化成 本 。根據 凱 基投顧 引述產 業數 據預
估，2027年客製化晶片出貨量將年增 92%至
1,446萬顆 ，首度超 越繪圖處理器 (GPU)的
1,129萬顆 ，顯示AI晶片市場正由 GPU主導
逐步轉向多元化發展。
► 在客製化晶片方面 ，谷歌持續擴大自研張量
處理器 (TPU)的應用與對外供應 ，亞馬遜亦
積極拓展自研AI晶片(Trainium)的雲端運算服
務，預估兩者合計將佔 2027年客製化晶片出
貨量約80%。
► 隨客製 化晶 片需 求 快速增 加 ，相 關晶 片設
計、先 進製 程及 先 進封裝 需求可 望同 步提
升，涉及上述業務發展的台股供應鏈 (註)有機
會受到市場關注。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 2024年全球工廠運作的工業機器人約 470萬
台，為10年前的 3倍，主要為韓國 、中國和
日本等製造業大國將其運用於汽車 、電子和
金屬加工等產業 ，並因而維持成本和品質優
勢，為競爭力重要來源之一 。雖然美國工業
機器人的普及程度仍低於亞洲主要製造業國
家，但近年積極尋求重建工業產能 、將關鍵
供應鏈遷回美國國內以及擴大半導體和製藥
生產，有可能讓美國逐步調整工業和製造層
面的策略方向。
► 傳統工業機器人與人形機器人在外觀設計與
應用場景上有明顯差異 。工業機器人通常部
署於高度標準化的環境 ，以執行範圍相對有
限且重複性高的特定作業為主 ，而人形機器
人則被設計用於多變的環境 ，並經常需要與
人類協同工作 ，因此需結合移動能力 、感知
能力與靈巧操作能力 ，才能夠執行多樣化任
務，而非受限單一功能。
工業機器人技術相對成熟且部署速度較快全球運作中的工業機器人數量穩定成長
工業機器人技術成熟且穩定成長，為製造業強國的重要競爭力來源
投資焦點

### Page 7

7
投資焦點人形機器人
► 人 形機 器 人 主 要由 結 合半 導 體 與AI 模 型的
「大腦」，並由感測器 、執行器 、線材、連
接器及輕量化電池驅動的機身所組成 ，可透
過感知環境資訊 、思考並進行協調的實體動
作。投行預估至2030年，執行器將佔整體人
形機器人成本超過 50%，為價值含量最高的
核心零組件 ，麥肯錫公司甚至認為執行器與
感測器可能成為未來供應鏈中的關鍵瓶頸。
► 機器人在實體世界中執行任務時 ，任何延遲
或連線中斷都可能帶來重大風險 ，因此相較
純數位的 AI應用 ，對即時運算 (邊緣運算 )能
力的要求更高 。而近幾年邊緣運算軟硬體能
力大幅提升 ，使本地端設備得以部署數十億
參數的 AI模型 ，並將反應延遲縮短至約 0.3
秒，使機器人能夠即時感知 、決策與行動 。
此外，過去6年風險資本對機器人、感測器及
IT 硬體的每年平均投資約 100 億美元 ， 但
2026年僅上半年即已達 280億美元 ，暗示市
場對機器人產業潛力的期待有別於過往。
機器人技術進展快速並開始吸引風險資本投資人形機器人運作有賴AI模型與大量機身零組件整合

### Page 8

8
股市
◆ 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，公債殖利率走高壓抑股債市場近期動能 。然而包括油價自高檔回落使
通膨壓力稍緩 ，以及美股第三季獲利年增率仍為雙位數成長 ，股市表現有支撐 。市場持續觀察AI資
本支出是否帶來獲利成長 ，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場動盪 ，可分散配置於不同國家與產業 ，降
低市場波動影響 。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評價面壓力等紛擾帶來負面影響 ，可布局防
禦股抗震，長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
◆ 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
◆ 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間 ，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展 ，加上9月製造業及服務業PMI優於預期，殖利率偏高環境建議

以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走升時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流
與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
◆ 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
◆ 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
◆ 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
◆ 中東地緣衝突加劇推升油價，短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
◆ 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 396: Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less transparent era. So, is that
a positive or negative development, and what could it mean for markets and the
economy? We speak with Former Fed governors Donald Kohn and Stephen Miran
as well as GS’ Jan Hatzius who agree that rigid “Odyssean” forward guidance that
commits policymakers to a pre-set path risks policy mistakes and is therefore rarely
appropriate. But they somewhat disagree about the benefits of great er transparency

around the Fed’s reaction function. Hatzius and Kohn argue that such transparency
helps markets better anticipate and price in policy changes, accelerating monetary
policy transmission. But Miran believes that the line between committing to a path
and being transparent is clearer in theory than in practice. And while GS economists
and strategists find that reduc ed transparency leads to increased market volatility,
the question is whether this just increases the market’s noise or also improves the

market’s signal. Hatzius argues largely for the former and Miran for the latter.
Jan Hatzius, Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research, Goldman Sachs
“Markets price what they think the Fed will do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true even if the Fed
obscures its reaction function… market guesses about the Fed’s next moves will simply become worse, leading to more
volatility... and importantly, volatility that serves no constructive economic purpose.”

Donald Kohn, former Vice Chairman of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, Robert V . Roosa
Chair in International Economics and Senior Fellow, Brookings Institution
“I sympathize with Chairman Warsh to some extent. [But] the real problem with Warsh’s approach is his reluctance to articulate
how the FOMC sees the economy evolving… explaining the Fed’s thinking about the economic outlook is essential in helping
markets act as a stabilizing force and is a core component of accountability.”
